  - [Lvl 1] Relentless Endurance: When you are reduced to 0 hit points but not killed outright, you can drop to 1 hit point instead. Tracked as a once-per-long-rest use below.
  - [Lvl 1] Savage Attacks: When you score a critical hit with a melee weapon attack, you can roll one of the weapon's damage dice one additional time and add it to the extra damage of the critical hit.
  - [Lvl 1] Speed: 30 ft. walking
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):

--- Barbarian ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): Rage-fueled front-line brawler. You charge in first, soak hits, and hit back harder — best for those who want simple, brutal, high-damage melee.
Hit Die: d12
Subclass unlock level: 3
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants strSaveProf
  - Grants conSaveProf
  - Grants armorProf: Light Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Medium Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Shields
  - Grants weaponProf: All Simple Weapons
  - Grants weaponProf: All Martial Weapons
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d12
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 12 + your Constitution modifier
  - [Lvl 1] Rage: As a bonus action, rage for up to 1 minute. You have advantage on Strength checks and saving throws, deal +2 damage with Strength melee attacks, and resist bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing damage. You cannot cast or concentrate on spells while raging.
  - [Lvl 1] Unarmored Defense: AC equals 10 + DEX mod + CON mod while wearing no armor.
  - [Lvl 2] Reckless Attack: Gain advantage on melee weapon attack rolls using Strength during your turn.
  - [Lvl 2] Danger Sense: Advantage on Dexterity saving throws against effects you can see.
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 5] Extra Attack: Attack twice whenever you take the Attack action on your turn.
  - [Lvl 5] Fast Movement: Speed increases by 10 feet while not wearing heavy armor.
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Primal Champion: Strength and Constitution scores increase by 4, up to a maximum of 24.
  - [Lvl 3] Primal Knowledge (Optional): When you reach 3rd level and again at 10th level, you gain proficiency in one skill of your choice from the list of skills available to barbarians at 1st level. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 7] Instinctive Pounce (Optional): As part of the bonus action you take to enter your rage, you can move up to half your speed. This is an optional class feature.
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Rage: max 2, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 3] Rage: max 3, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 6] Rage: max 4, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 12] Rage: max 5, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 17] Rage: max 6, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 20] Rage: max 99, reset: long rest

--- Monk ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): Fast, unarmored striker. Stunning strikes, mobility, and ki-powered tricks — best for those who want speed and style over armor.
Hit Die: d8
Subclass unlock level: 3
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants strSaveProf
  - Grants dexSaveProf
  - Grants weaponProf: All Simple Weapons
  - Grants weaponProf: Shortsword
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d8
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 8 + CON_mod
  - [Lvl 1] Unarmored Defense: AC equals 10 + DEX mod + WIS mod when wearing no armor.
  - [Lvl 1] Martial Arts: Use Dexterity instead of Strength for monk weapons and unarmed strikes. Your unarmed strike deals 1d4 damage, and after attacking with a monk weapon or unarmed strike you can make one unarmed strike as a bonus action.
  - [Lvl 2] Ki: Fuel ki features such as Flurry of Blows, Patient Defense, and Step of the Wind.
  - [Lvl 2] Unarmored Movement: Speed increases by 10 ft while not wearing armor or wielding a shield.
  - [Lvl 3] Deflect Missiles: Use reaction to reduce damage from ranged weapon attacks.
  - [Lvl 4] Slow Fall: Use reaction to reduce falling damage by 5x Monk level.
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 5] Extra Attack: Attack twice whenever you take the Attack action.
  - [Lvl 5] Stunning Strike: Spend 1 ki point to attempt to stun a target hit by a melee weapon attack.
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Perfect Self: Regain 4 ki points if you have 0 ki points when initiative is rolled.
  - [Lvl 2] Dedicated Weapon (Optional): After a short/long rest, touch one simple/martial weapon you are proficient with (no heavy/special) to count it as a monk weapon. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 3] Ki-Fueled Attack (Optional): If you spend 1+ ki as part of your action, make one unarmed strike or monk weapon attack as a bonus action. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 4] Quickened Healing (Optional): As an action, spend 2 ki and roll a Martial Arts die; regain that + proficiency bonus hit points. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 5] Focused Aim (Optional): When you miss, spend 1-3 ki to add +2 per ki spent to the attack roll. This is an optional class feature.

--- Bard ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): Charismatic jack-of-all-trades. You talk, sneak, cast, and inspire the party — best for those who want to do a little of everything and be the face.
Hit Die: d8
Caster: full
Subclass unlock level: 3
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants dexSaveProf
  - Grants chaSaveProf
  - Grants armorProf: Light Armor
  - Grants weaponProf: All Simple Weapons
  - Grants weaponProf: Hand Crossbow
  - Grants weaponProf: Longsword
  - Grants weaponProf: Rapier
  - Grants weaponProf: Shortsword
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d8
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 8 + CON_mod
  - [Lvl 1] Spellcasting: You use Charisma for bard spells; choose your cantrips and spells in the Spells step.
  - [Lvl 1] Bardic Inspiration: As a bonus action, give a creature within 60 feet that can hear you one d6. Within 10 minutes, it can add the die to one ability check, attack roll, or saving throw. Uses: your Charisma modifier per long rest (minimum once).
  - [Lvl 2] Jack of All Trades: Add half your proficiency bonus to any ability check that doesn't already include it.
  - [Lvl 2] Song of Rest: Perform during a short rest to help revitalize allies, restoring extra HP.
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 5] Font of Inspiration: Regain all uses of Bardic Inspiration when you finish a short or long rest.
  - [Lvl 6] Countercharm: Use musical notes or words to disrupt mind-influencing effects.
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Superior Inspiration: Regain one use of Bardic Inspiration if you have none when you roll initiative.
  - [Lvl 2] Magical Inspiration (Optional): If a creature has a Bardic Inspiration die from you and casts a spell that restores hit points or deals damage, the creature can roll that die and add the number rolled as a bonus to the hit points regained or the damage dealt. The die is then lost. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 4] Bardic Versatility (Optional): Whenever you reach a level that grants Ability Score Improvement, replace one Expertise skill with another proficient skill, or replace one Bard cantrip with another Bard cantrip. This is an optional class feature.
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Bardic Inspiration: max {"type":"expr","text":"max(1, {{chaMod}})"}, reset: long rest

--- Cleric ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): Divine full caster in armor. You heal, protect, and smite for your god — best for those who want magic with staying power and a sacred mission.
Hit Die: d8
Caster: full
Subclass unlock level: 1
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants wisSaveProf
  - Grants chaSaveProf
  - Grants armorProf: Light Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Medium Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Shields
  - Grants weaponProf: All Simple Weapons
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d8
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 8 + CON_mod
  - [Lvl 1] Spellcasting: You use Wisdom for cleric spells. Prepare your spells and choose your cantrips in the Spells step.
  - [Lvl 2] Channel Divinity: Gain divine energy from your deity to power magical effects.
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 5] Destroy Undead: Turned undead of low CR are instantly destroyed upon failing saving throw.
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 10] Divine Intervention: Call on your deity to intervene on your behalf once per 7 days on success.
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Divine Intervention Improvement: Divine Intervention succeeds automatically without rolling.
  - [Lvl 2] Harness Divine Power (Optional): Expend a use of Channel Divinity as a bonus action to regain one expended spell slot of level no higher than half your proficiency bonus (rounded up). Usable once (2nd), twice (6th), thrice (18th). Regain uses on long rest. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 4] Cantrip Versatility (Optional): Whenever you reach a level that grants Ability Score Improvement, replace one Cleric cantrip with another Cleric cantrip. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 8] Blessed Strikes (Optional): When a creature takes damage from your cantrip or weapon attack, deal an extra 1d8 radiant damage (once per turn). Replaces Divine Strike or Potent Spellcasting. This is an optional class feature.
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  - [Lvl 2] Channel Divinity: max 1, reset: short or long rest
  - [Lvl 6] Channel Divinity: max 2, reset: short or long rest
  - [Lvl 18] Channel Divinity: max 3, reset: short or long rest

  - [Lvl 2] Wild Shape: Magically assume the shape of a beast you have seen before.

  - [Lvl 18] Timeless Body: Age slower; for every 10 years that pass, your body ages only 1 year.
  - [Lvl 18] Beast Spells: Cast many spells while remaining in Wild Shape forms.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Archdruid: Use Wild Shape an unlimited number of times and ignore non-costly spell components.
  - [Lvl 2] Wild Companion (Optional): Expend a use of Wild Shape as an action to cast Find Familiar without material components; the familiar is a fey and disappears after hours equal to half your druid level. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 4] Cantrip Versatility (Optional): Whenever you reach a level that grants Ability Score Improvement, replace one Druid cantrip with another Druid cantrip. This is an optional class feature.

--- Paladin ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): Holy warrior bound by oath. Heavy armor, burst smite damage, and protective auras — best for those who want righteous melee with conviction.
Hit Die: d10
Caster: half
Subclass unlock level: 3
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants wisSaveProf
  - Grants chaSaveProf
  - Grants armorProf: Light Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Medium Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Heavy Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Shields
  - Grants weaponProf: All Simple Weapons
  - Grants weaponProf: All Martial Weapons
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d10
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 10 + CON_mod
  - [Lvl 1] Divine Sense: As an action, detect celestials, fiends, undead, and consecrated or desecrated places within 60 feet until the end of your next turn. Uses: 1 + your Charisma modifier per long rest.
  - [Lvl 1] Lay on Hands: You have a healing pool of 5 hit points. As an action, restore hit points from the pool or spend 5 points to cure one poison or disease.
  - [Lvl 2] Spellcasting: Cast prepared paladin spells using Charisma.
  - [Lvl 2] Divine Smite: Expend spell slots to deal extra radiant damage on a melee hit.
  - [Lvl 3] Divine Health: Immunity to disease.
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 5] Extra Attack: Attack twice whenever you take the Attack action.
  - [Lvl 6] Aura of Protection: Allies within 10 feet gain a bonus to saving throws equal to Charisma modifier.
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 10] Aura of Courage: Allies within 10 feet cannot be frightened.
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 14] Cleansing Touch: End one spell on yourself or a willing creature a number of times equal to Charisma modifier.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 2] Blessed Warrior (Optional): Learn two Cleric cantrips (Cha-based, count as Paladin spells); replace one per Paladin level. This is an optional fighting style.
  - [Lvl 2] Blind Fighting (Optional): You have blindsight with a range of 10 feet. You can see invisible creatures in range unless hidden. This is an optional fighting style.
  - [Lvl 2] Interception (Optional): When a creature you can see hits another target within 5 feet of you, use your reaction to reduce the damage by 1d10 + proficiency bonus (min 0). Must wield shield or simple/martial weapon. This is an optional fighting style.
  - [Lvl 3] Harness Divine Power (Optional): Expend Channel Divinity as a bonus action to regain one spell slot of level no higher than half proficiency (rounded up). Usable once (3rd), twice (7th), thrice (15th). This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 4] Martial Versatility (Optional): Whenever you reach a level that grants Ability Score Improvement, replace a fighting style you know. This is an optional class feature.

--- Ranger ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): Wilderness hunter and tracker. Favored enemies, exploration mastery, and skirmish archery — best for those who want a self-reliant outdoors specialist.
Hit Die: d10
Caster: half
Subclass unlock level: 3
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants strSaveProf
  - Grants dexSaveProf
  - Grants armorProf: Light Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Medium Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Shields
  - Grants weaponProf: All Simple Weapons
  - Grants weaponProf: All Martial Weapons
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d10
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 10 + CON_mod
  - [Lvl 1] Favored Enemy: Choose a favored enemy type. You have advantage on Survival checks to track it and Intelligence checks to recall information about it; you learn one language spoken by that type.
  - [Lvl 1] Natural Explorer: Choose a favored terrain. While traveling there, gain the listed navigation, tracking, foraging, and movement benefits.
  - [Lvl 2] Spellcasting: Cast ranger spells using Wisdom modifier.
  - [Lvl 3] Primeval Awareness: Focus sense to detect nearby magical creatures.
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 5] Extra Attack: Attack twice whenever you take the Attack action.
  - [Lvl 8] Land's Stride: Moving through nonmagical difficult terrain costs no extra movement.
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 10] Hide in Plain Sight: Spend 1 minute creating camouflage to gain +10 to Stealth checks.
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 14] Vanish: Use Hide as a bonus action and cannot be tracked by nonmagical means.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 18] Feral Senses: Attack creatures you can't see without disadvantage.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Foe Slayer: Add Wisdom modifier to attack or damage roll against a favored enemy once per turn.
  - [Lvl 2] Blind Fighting (Optional): You have blindsight with a range of 10 feet. This is an optional fighting style.
  - [Lvl 2] Druidic Warrior (Optional): Learn two Druid cantrips (Wis-based, count as Ranger spells); replace one per Ranger level. This is an optional fighting style.
  - [Lvl 2] Thrown Weapon Fighting (Optional): Draw a thrown weapon as part of the attack; +2 damage on ranged thrown-weapon hits. This is an optional fighting style.
  - [Lvl 2] Spellcasting Focus (Optional): You can use a druidic focus for your Ranger spells. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 3] Primal Awareness (Optional): Replaces Primeval Awareness. Learn Speak with Animals (3rd), Beast Sense (5th), Speak with Plants (9th), Locate Creature (13th), Commune with Nature (17th); cast each once free per long rest. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 4] Martial Versatility (Optional): Whenever you reach a level that grants Ability Score Improvement, replace a fighting style you know. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 10] Nature's Veil (Optional): Replaces Hide in Plain Sight. As a bonus action become invisible (with gear) until start of next turn; usable proficiency times/rest. This is an optional class feature.
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  (none)
Subclass access (dropdownAccess):
  - subclass (lvl 3): Beast Master Conclave | Drakewarden | Fey Wanderer | Gloom Stalker Conclave | Horizon Walker Conclave | Hunter Conclave | Monster Slayer Conclave | Swarmkeeper

--- Warlock ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): Pact-bound caster with endlessly renewable tricks. Eldritch Blast plus invocations on a short-rest rhythm — best for those who like a dark bargain.
Hit Die: d8
Caster: pact
Subclass unlock level: 1
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants wisSaveProf
  - Grants chaSaveProf
  - Grants armorProf: Light Armor
  - Grants weaponProf: All Simple Weapons
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d8
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 8 + CON_mod
  - [Lvl 1] Pact Magic: You use Charisma for warlock spells. Choose two cantrips and two 1st-level spells in the Spells step; your spell slots return when you finish a short or long rest.
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 11] Mystic Arcanum (6th level): When you gain this feature, you learn a 6th-level warlock spell that you can cast at will.
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 13] Mystic Arcanum (7th level): When you gain this feature, you learn a 7th-level warlock spell that you can cast at will.
  - [Lvl 15] Mystic Arcanum (8th level): When you gain this feature, you learn an 8th-level warlock spell that you can cast at will.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 17] Mystic Arcanum (9th level): When you gain this feature, you learn a 9th-level warlock spell that you can cast at will.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Eldritch Master: Entreat patron for 1 minute to regain all Pact Magic spell slots once per long rest.
  - [Lvl 3] Pact of the Talisman (Optional): Your patron gives you an amulet. When the wearer fails an ability check, add d4 (potentially turning failure to success), usable proficiency times/rest. 1-hour ceremony replaces a lost talisman. This is an optional Pact Boon.
  - [Lvl 4] Eldritch Versatility (Optional): Whenever you reach a level that grants Ability Score Improvement, replace one Warlock cantrip, one Pact Boon option, or (12th+) one Mystic Arcanum spell. If this makes you ineligible for invocations, replace those too. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 5] Eldritch Invocation: Eldritch Mind (Optional): Advantage on Constitution saves to maintain concentration. This is an optional invocation.
  - [Lvl 5] Eldritch Invocation: Far Scribe (Optional): Prerequisite: 5th level, Pact of the Tome. A page in your Book of Shadows holds names (up to proficiency); cast Sending to a named creature without slot/components by writing the message (reply appears on page, fades in 1 min). Erase names by touch. This is an optional invocation.
  - [Lvl 5] Eldritch Invocation: Investment of the Chain Master (Optional): Prerequisite: Pact of the Chain. Your familiar gains 40-ft fly or swim speed; bonus action to command Attack; attacks count as magical; saves use your DC; reaction grants it resistance when damaged. This is an optional invocation.
  - [Lvl 5] Eldritch Invocation: Rebuke of the Talisman (Optional): Prerequisite: Pact of the Talisman. When the wearer is hit by an attacker you see within 30 ft, use your reaction to deal proficiency-bonus psychic damage and push it 10 ft. This is an optional invocation.
  - [Lvl 5] Eldritch Invocation: Undying Servitude (Optional): Prerequisite: 5th level. Cast Animate Dead once without a slot (long rest to reuse). This is an optional invocation.
  - [Lvl 7] Eldritch Invocation: Protection of the Talisman (Optional): Prerequisite: 7th level, Pact of the Talisman. When the wearer fails a save, add d4 (proficiency times/rest). This is an optional invocation.
  - [Lvl 9] Eldritch Invocation: Gift of the Protectors (Optional): Prerequisite: 9th level, Pact of the Tome. Names on the page (up to proficiency); a named creature reduced to 0 HP drops to 1 HP instead (once until long rest). Erase names by touch. This is an optional invocation.
  - [Lvl 12] Eldritch Invocation: Bond of the Talisman (Optional): Prerequisite: 12th level, Pact of the Talisman. You or the wearer can action-teleport to the nearest unoccupied space by the other (same plane), usable proficiency times/rest. This is an optional invocation.
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  (none)
Subclass access (dropdownAccess):
  - subclass (lvl 1): The Archfey | The Celestial | The Fathomless | The Fiend | The Genie | The Great Old One | The Hexblade | The Undead | The Undying

--- Wizard ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): The prepared scholar of magic. The biggest spellbook in the game — best for those who love planning, utility, and always having the right spell.
Hit Die: d6
Caster: full
Subclass unlock level: 2
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants intSaveProf
  - Grants wisSaveProf
  - Grants weaponProf: Dagger
  - Grants weaponProf: Dart
  - Grants weaponProf: Sling
  - Grants weaponProf: Quarterstaff
  - Grants weaponProf: Light Crossbow
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d6
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 6 + CON_mod
  - [Lvl 1] Spellcasting: You use Intelligence for wizard spells. Your spellbook starts with six 1st-level wizard spells; choose your cantrips and prepared spells in the Spells step.
  - [Lvl 1] Arcane Recovery: Once per day when you finish a short rest, recover expended spell slots with a combined level up to half your wizard level, rounded up. No recovered slot can be 6th level or higher.
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 18] Spell Mastery: Cast chosen 1st and 2nd-level spells at will without expending slots.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Signature Spells: Two 3rd-level spells are always prepared and castable once per short rest without slots.
  - [Lvl 3] Cantrip Formulas (Optional): Each long rest, after consulting your spellbook formulas, replace one Wizard cantrip with another Wizard cantrip. This is an optional class feature.
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  (none)
Subclass access (dropdownAccess):
  - subclass (lvl 2): Bladesinging | Chronurgy Magic | Graviturgy Magic | Order of Scribes | School of Abjuration | School of Conjuration | School of Divination | School of Enchantment | School of Evocation | School of Illusion | School of Necromancy | School of Transmutation | War Magic
Choices (choiceGroups):
  - Wizard Skill Proficiencies (choose 2, lvl 1)
      * Arcana
          > Grants arcanaProf
      * History
          > Grants historyProf
      * Insight
          > Grants insightProf
      * Investigation
          > Grants investigationProf
      * Medicine
          > Grants medicineProf
      * Religion
          > Grants religionProf
  - 1st-level spells (choose 2, lvl 18) [spell picker: wizard lvl 1]
      * Choose 2 spells — Opens the spell list.
  - 2nd-level spells (choose 2, lvl 18) [spell picker: wizard lvl 2]
      * Choose 2 spells — Opens the spell list.
  - 3rd-level spells (choose 2, lvl 20) [spell picker: wizard lvl 3]
      * Choose 2 spells — Opens the spell list.
Catalog link: class:wizard

--- Sorcerer ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): Instinctive arcane blaster. Few spells known, but metamagic bends them — best for those who want explosive, flexible casting.
Hit Die: d6
Caster: full
Subclass unlock level: 1
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants conSaveProf
  - Grants chaSaveProf
  - Grants weaponProf: Dagger
  - Grants weaponProf: Dart
  - Grants weaponProf: Sling
  - Grants weaponProf: Quarterstaff
  - Grants weaponProf: Light Crossbow
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d6
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 6 + CON_mod
  - [Lvl 1] Spellcasting: You use Charisma for sorcerer spells. Choose your cantrips and spells known in the Spells step.
  - [Lvl 2] Font of Magic: Gain Sorcery Points to transform spell slots or fuel Metamagic effects.
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Sorcerous Restoration: Regain 4 expended sorcery points whenever you finish a short rest.
  - [Lvl 3] Seeking Spell (Optional): When you miss a spell attack, spend 2 sorcery points to reroll the d20. Usable even with another Metamagic on the spell. This is an optional Metamagic.
  - [Lvl 3] Transmuted Spell (Optional): When you cast a spell dealing acid/cold/fire/lightning/poison/thunder, spend 1 sorcery point to change it to another listed type. This is an optional Metamagic.
  - [Lvl 4] Sorcerous Versatility (Optional): Whenever you reach a level that grants Ability Score Improvement, replace one Metamagic option or one Sorcerer cantrip. This is an optional class feature.
  - [Lvl 5] Magical Guidance (Optional): When you fail an ability check, spend 1 sorcery point to reroll the d20. This is an optional class feature.
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  - [Lvl 2] Sorcery Points: max 2, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 3] Sorcery Points: max 3, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 4] Sorcery Points: max 4, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 5] Sorcery Points: max 5, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 6] Sorcery Points: max 6, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 7] Sorcery Points: max 7, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 8] Sorcery Points: max 8, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 9] Sorcery Points: max 9, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 10] Sorcery Points: max 10, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 11] Sorcery Points: max 11, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 12] Sorcery Points: max 12, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 13] Sorcery Points: max 13, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 14] Sorcery Points: max 14, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 15] Sorcery Points: max 15, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 16] Sorcery Points: max 16, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 17] Sorcery Points: max 17, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 18] Sorcery Points: max 18, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 19] Sorcery Points: max 19, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 20] Sorcery Points: max 20, reset: long rest
Subclass access (dropdownAccess):
  - subclass (lvl 1): Aberrant Mind | Clockwork Soul | Divine Soul | Draconic Bloodline | Shadow Magic | Storm Sorcery | Wild Magic
Choices (choiceGroups):
  - Sorcerer Skill Proficiencies (choose 2, lvl 1)
      * Arcana
          > Grants arcanaProf
      * Deception
          > Grants deceptionProf
      * Insight
          > Grants insightProf
      * Intimidation
          > Grants intimidationProf
      * Persuasion
          > Grants persuasionProf
      * Religion
          > Grants religionProf
  - Metamagic â€” pick 2 (choose 2, lvl 3)
      * Careful Spell — Spend 1 sorcery point; chosen creatures automatically succeed on the spell's save. (Costs sorcery points â€” tracked, not auto-spent.)

--- Artificer ---
Flavor (site picker blurb): Magical inventor and tinkerer. Infuse gear, brew elixirs or pilot arcane armor — best for those who want tools, pets, and gadgets over raw spellpower.
Hit Die: d8
Caster: half
Subclass unlock level: 3
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  - Grants conSaveProf
  - Grants intSaveProf
  - Grants armorProf: Light Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Medium Armor
  - Grants armorProf: Shields
  - Grants weaponProf: All Simple Weapons
  - Grants toolProf: Thieves' Tools
  - Grants toolProf: Tinker's Tools
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Die: d8
  - [Lvl 1] Hit Points at 1st Level: 8 + your Constitution modifier
  - [Lvl 1] Firearm Proficiency (optional): If your campaign uses firearms and your artificer has been exposed to them, you are proficient with them.
  - [Lvl 1] Magical Tinkering: Touch a Tiny nonmagical object to give it light, a recorded message, an odor/sound, or a static visual effect (Int mod objects max).
  - [Lvl 1] Spellcasting: Prepare Int mod + half artificer level spells (min 1); cast through thieves' tools or artisan's tools (infused items count as a focus after 2nd level). Ritual casting for prepared ritual spells.
  - [Lvl 2] Infuse Item: After a long rest, imbue mundane items with learned infusions (4 known, 2 infused at 2nd level, growing with level). Each infusion in one object; one infusion per object.
  - [Lvl 3] Artificer Specialist: Choose Alchemist, Armorer, Artillerist, or Battle Smith.
  - [Lvl 3] The Right Tool for the Job: With tools in hand, magically create one set of artisan's tools in 1 hour (vanishes when reused).
  - [Lvl 4] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 5] Artificer Specialist feature: Your specialist subclass grants additional features at 5th level (and again at 9th and 15th).
  - [Lvl 8] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 6] Tool Expertise: Doubled proficiency bonus on checks using a tool you're proficient with.
  - [Lvl 7] Flash of Genius: As a reaction, add your Int modifier to an ability check or save you or a creature within 30 ft makes (Int mod uses per long rest).
  - [Lvl 9] Artificer Specialist feature: Your specialist subclass grants additional features at 9th level (and again at 15th).
  - [Lvl 12] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 10] Magic Item Adept: Attune to up to 4 magic items; craft common/uncommon items in 1/4 time for 1/2 gold.
  - [Lvl 11] Spell-Storing Item: After a long rest, store a 1st/2nd-level artificer spell (1 action) in a weapon or focus; usable 2 x Int mod times (min twice).
  - [Lvl 14] Magic Item Savant: Attune to up to 5 magic items; ignore class/race/spell/level requirements.
  - [Lvl 15] Artificer Specialist feature: Your specialist subclass grants its final features at 15th level.
  - [Lvl 16] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 18] Magic Item Master: Attune to up to 6 magic items.
  - [Lvl 19] Ability Score Improvement: Increase one ability score by 2, two ability scores by 1 each, or take a feat instead.
  - [Lvl 20] Soul of Artifice: +1 to all saves per attuned item; use a reaction to end an infusion and drop to 1 HP instead of 0.
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  - [Lvl 7] Flash of Genius: max {"type":"expr","text":"max(1, {{intMod}})"}, reset: long rest
  - [Lvl 11] Spell-Storing Item: max {"type":"expr","text":"max(2, 2 * {{intMod}})"}, reset: long rest

  - subclass (lvl 3): Alchemist | Armorer | Artillerist | Battle Smith

========================================================================
SUBCLASSES (grouped by class)
========================================================================

**** Barbarian — subclasses (8), unlock lvl 3 ****
--- Path of the Battlerager (Barbarian) [Player's Handbook] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Battlerager Armor: When you choose this path at 3rd level, you gain the ability to use spiked armor as a weapon. While you are wearing spiked armor and are raging, you can use a bonus action to make one melee weapon attack with your armor spikes against a target within 5 feet of you. If the attack hits, the spikes deal 1d4 piercing damage. You use your Strength modifier for the attack and damage rolls. Additionally, when you use the Attack action to grapple a creature, the target takes 3 piercing damage if your grapple check succeeds. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:battlerager]
  - [Lvl 6] Reckless Abandon: Beginning at 6th level, when you use Reckless Attack while raging, you also gain temporary hit points equal to your Constitution modifier (minimum of 1). They vanish if any of them are left when your rage ends. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:battlerager]
  - [Lvl 10] Battlerager Charge: Beginning at 10th level, you can take the Dash action as a bonus action while you are raging. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:battlerager]
  - [Lvl 14] Spiked Retribution: Starting at 14th level, when a creature within 5 feet of you hits you with a melee attack, the attacker takes 3 piercing damage if you are raging, aren't incapacitated, and are wearing spiked armor. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:battlerager]
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  (none)
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  (none)
Choices (choiceGroups):
  (none)
Catalog link: subclass:path-of-the-battlerager

--- Path of the Berserker (Barbarian) [Player's Handbook] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Frenzy: Your frenzy gives you a number of benefits that depend on the type of frenzy you choose. Bear (berserk), Totem (free), or Beast (frenzied attack).
  - [Lvl 6] Mindless Rage: Beginning at 6th level, you can't be charmed or frightened while raging. If you are charmed or frightened when you enter your rage, the effect is suspended for the duration of the rage. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:berserker]
  - [Lvl 10] Intimidating Presence: Beginning at 10th level, you can use your action to frighten someone with your menacing presence. When you do so, choose one creature that you can see within 30 feet of you. If the creature can see or hear you, it must succeed on a Wisdom saving throw (DC equal to 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Charisma modifier) or be frightened of you until the end of your next turn. On subsequent turns, you can use your action to extend the duration of this effect on the frightened creature until the end of your next turn. This effect ends if the creature ends its turn out of line of sight or more than 60 feet away from you. If the creature succeeds on its saving throw, you can't use this feature on that creature again for 24 hours. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:berserker]
  - [Lvl 14] Retaliation: Starting at 14th level, when you take damage from a creature that is within 5 feet of you, you can use your reaction to make a melee weapon attack against that creature. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:berserker]
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  (none)
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  (none)
Choices (choiceGroups):
  - Frenzy type (choose 1, lvl 3)
      * Bear — Berserk
      * Totem — Free
      * Beast — Frenzied attack
Catalog link: subclass:path-of-the-berserker

--- Path of the Totem Warrior (Barbarian) [Player's Handbook] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Spirit Seeker: Yours is a path that seeks attunement with the natural world, giving you a kinship with beasts. At 3rd level when you adopt this path, you gain the ability to cast the Beast Sense and Speak with Animals spells, but only as rituals. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:totem-warrior]
  - [Lvl 3] Totem Spirit: Your spirit grants you magic that comes from one of the totem spirits of your people: Bear, Eagle, or Wolf.
  - [Lvl 6] Aspect of the Beast: Your choice of totem spirit grants you an aspect of the beast. Your spirit's Aspect of the Beast is one of the following.
  - [Lvl 10] Spirit Walker: At 10th level, you can cast the Commune with Nature spell, but only as a ritual. When you do so, a spiritual version of one of the animals you chose for Totem Spirit or Aspect of the Beast appears to you to convey the information you seek. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:totem-warrior]
  - [Lvl 14] Totemic Attunement: Your choice of totem spirit grants you the ability to attune to a different totem spirit from the following list each day.

--- Path of the Ancestral Guardian (Barbarian) [Xanathar's Guide to Everything] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Ancestral Protectors: Choose a spirit that lends you aid: Ancestor, Spirits of the Land, or Spirits of the Dead.
  - [Lvl 6] Spirit Shield: Beginning at 6th level, the guardian spirits that aid you can provide supernatural protection to those you defend. If you are raging and another creature you can see within 30 feet of you takes damage, you can use your reaction to reduce that damage by 2d6. When you reach certain levels in this class, you can reduce the damage by more: by 3d6 at 10th level and by 4d6 at 14th level. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:ancestral-guardian]
  - [Lvl 10] Consult the Spirits: At 10th level, you gain the ability to consult with your ancestral spirits. When you do so, you cast the Augury or Clairvoyance spell, without using a spell slot or material components. Rather than creating a spherical sensor, this use of clairvoyance invisibly summons one of your ancestral spirits to the chosen location. Wisdom is your spellcasting ability for these spells. After you cast either spell in this way, you can't use this feature again until you finish a short or long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:ancestral-guardian]
  - [Lvl 14] Vengeful Ancestors: At 14th level, your ancestral spirits grow powerful enough to retaliate. When you use your Spirit Shield to reduce the damage of an attack, the attacker takes an amount of force damage that your Spirit Shield prevents. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:ancestral-guardian]
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  (none)
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  (none)
Choices (choiceGroups):
  - Guardian spirit (choose 1, lvl 3)
      * Ancestor — an elder of your bloodline
      * Spirits of the Land — nature spirits
      * Spirits of the Dead — spirits bound to death
Catalog link: subclass:path-of-the-ancestral-guardian

--- Path of the Storm Herald (Barbarian) [Xanathar's Guide to Everything] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Storm Aura: You gain magic from your spirit that manifests as an aura. Your storm aura comes from one of the following storm types.
  - [Lvl 6] Storm Soul: At 6th level, the storm grants you benefits even when your aura isn't active. The benefits are based on the environment you chose for your Storm Aura. Desert. You gain resistance to fire damage, and you don’t suffer the effects of extreme heat, as described in the Dungeon Master's Guide. Moreover, as an action, you can touch a flammable object that isn't being worn or carried by anyone else and set it on fire. Sea. You gain resistance to lightning damage, and you can breathe underwater. You also gain a swimming speed of 30 feet. Tundra. You gain resistance to cold damage, and you don’t suffer the effects of extreme cold, as described in the Dungeon Master's Guide. Moreover, as an action, you can touch water and turn a 5-foot cube of it into ice, which melts after 1 minute. This action fails if a creature is in the cube. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:storm-herald]
  - [Lvl 10] Shielding Storm: At 10th level, you learn to use your mastery of the storm to protect others. Each creature of your choice has the damage resistance you gained from the Storm Soul feature while the creature is in your Storm Aura. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:storm-herald]
  - [Lvl 14] Raging Storm: At 14th level, the power of the storm you channel grows mightier, lashing out at your foes. The effect is based on the environment you chose for your Storm Aura. Desert. Immediately after a creature in your aura hits you with an attack, you can use your reaction to force that creature to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failed save, the creature takes fire damage equal to half your Barbarian level. Sea. When you hit a creature in your aura with an attack, you can use your reaction to force that creature to make a Strength saving throw. On a failed save, the creature is knocked prone, as if struck by a wave. Tundra. Whenever the effect of your Storm Aura is activated, you can choose one creature you can see in the aura. That creature must succeed on a Strength saving throw, or its speed is reduced to 0 until the start of your next turn, as magical frost covers it. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:storm-herald]
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  (none)
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  (none)
Choices (choiceGroups):
  - Storm aura (choose 1, lvl 3)
      * Desert
      * Sea
      * Tundra
Catalog link: subclass:path-of-the-storm-herald

--- Path of the Zealot Herald (Barbarian) [Xanathar's Guide to Everything] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Divine Fury: Starting when you choose this path at 3rd level, you can channel divine fury into your weapon strikes. While you're raging, the first creature you hit on each of your turns with a weapon attack takes extra damage equal to 1d6 + half your Barbarian level. The extra damage is necrotic or radiant; you choose the type of damage when you gain this feature. Warrior of the Gods At 3rd level, your soul is marked for endless battle. If a spell, such as Raise Dead, has the sole effect of restoring you to life (but not undeath), the caster doesn't need material components to cast the spell on you. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:zealot]
  - [Lvl 3] Warrior of Gods: (no description on site)
  - [Lvl 6] Fanatical Focus: Starting at 6th level, the divine power that fuels your rage can protect you. If you fail a saving throw while raging, you can reroll it, and you must use the new roll. You can use this ability only once per rage. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:zealot]
  - [Lvl 10] Zealous Presence: At 10th level, you learn to channel divine power to inspire zealotry in others. As a bonus action, you unleash a battle cry infused with divine energy. Up to ten other creatures of your choice within 60 feet of you that can hear you gain advantage on attack rolls and saving throws until the start of your next turn. Once you use this feature, you can’t use it again until you finish a long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:zealot]
  - [Lvl 14] Rage Beyond Death: Beginning at 14th level, the divine power that fuels your rage allows you to shrug off fatal blows. While you're raging, having 0 hit points doesn’t knock you unconscious. You still must make death saving throws, and you suffer the normal effects of taking damage while at 0 hit points. However, if you would die due to failing death saving throws, you don’t die until your rage ends, and you die then only if you still have 0 hit points. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:zealot]

--- Path of the Beast (Barbarian) [Tasha's Cauldron of Everything] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Form of the Beast: You gain the ability to assume a beast form: Bear, Eagle, or Wolf.
  - [Lvl 6] Bestial Soul: Beginning at 6th level, the feral power within you increases, causing the natural weapons of your Form of the Beast to count as magical for the purpose of overcoming resistance and immunity to nonmagical attacks and damage. You can also alter your form to help you adapt to your surroundings. When you finish a short or long rest, choose one of the following benefits, which lasts until you finish a short or long rest: - You gain a swimming speed equal to your walking speed, and you can breathe underwater. - You gain a climbing speed equal to your walking speed, and you can climb difficult surfaces, including upside down on ceilings, without needing to make an ability check. - When you jump, you can make a Strength (Athletics) check and extend your jump by a number of feet equal to the check’s total. You can make this special check only once per turn. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:beast]
  - [Lvl 10] Infectious Fury: At 10th level, when you hit a creature with your natural weapons while you are raging, the beast within you can curse your target with rabid fury. The target must succeed on a Wisdom saving throw (DC equal to 8 + your Constitution modifier + your proficiency bonus) or suffer one of the following effects (your choice): - The target must use its reaction to make a melee attack against another creature of your choice that you can see. - Target takes 2d12 psychic damage. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:beast]
  - [Lvl 14] Call the Hunt: At 14th level, the beast within you grows so powerful that you can spread its ferocity to others and gain resilience from them joining your hunt. When you enter your rage, you can choose a number of other willing creatures you can see within 30 feet of you equal to your Constitution modifier (minimum of one creature). You gain 5 temporary hit points for each creature that accepts this feature. Until the rage ends, the chosen creatures can use the following benefit once on each of their turns: when the creature hits a target with an attack roll and deals damage to it, the creature can roll a d6 and gain a bonus to the damage equal to the number rolled. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus, and you regain all expended uses when you finish a long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/barbarian:beast]
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  (none)

  (none)
Choices (choiceGroups):
  - Totem form (choose 1, lvl 3)
      * Bear
      * Eagle
      * Wolf
Catalog link: subclass:path-of-the-beast

--- Banneret (Fighter) [Player's Handbook] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Rallying Cry: When you choose this archetype at 3rd level, you learn how to inspire your allies to fight on past their injuries. When you use your Second Wind feature, you can choose up to three creatures within 60 feet of you that are allied with you. Each one regains hit points equal to your fighter level, provided that the creature can see or hear you. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:banneret]
  - [Lvl 7] Royal Envoy: Knights of high standing are expected to conduct themselves with grace. At 7th level, you gain proficiency in the Persuasion skill. If you are already proficient in it, you gain proficiency in one of the following skills of your choice: Animal Handling, Insight, Intimidation, or Performance. Your proficiency bonus is doubled for any ability check you make that uses Persuasion. You receive this benefit regardless of the skill proficiency you gain from this feature. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:banneret]
  - [Lvl 10] Inspiring Surge (One Ally): Starting at 10th level, when you use your Action Surge feature, you can choose one creature within 60 feet of you that is allied with you. That creature can make one melee or ranged weapon attack with its reaction, provided that it can see or hear you. Starting at 18th level, you can choose two allies within 60 feet of you, rather than one. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:banneret]
  - [Lvl 15] Bulwark: Beginning at 15th level, you can extend the benefit of your Indomitable feature to an ally. When you decide to use Indomitable to reroll an Intelligence, a Wisdom, or a Charisma saving throw and you aren't incapacitated, you can choose one ally within 60 feet of you that also failed its saving throw against the same effect. If that creature can see or hear you, it can reroll its saving throw and must use the new roll. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:banneret]
  - [Lvl 18] Inspiring Surge (Two Allies): Starting at 10th level, when you use your Action Surge feature, you can choose one creature within 60 feet of you that is allied with you. That creature can make one melee or ranged weapon attack with its reaction, provided that it can see or hear you. Starting at 18th level, you can choose two allies within 60 feet of you, rather than one. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:banneret]

--- Battle Master (Fighter) [Player's Handbook] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Combat Superiority (d8): Your parting gift lets you manoeuvre on the battlefield. Choose the manoeuvres below; you gain one more whenever you gain a level here, and Counterspell at 15th.
  - [Lvl 3] Student of War: When you gain this feature, you gain proficiency with one tool of your choice.
  - [Lvl 7] Know Your Enemy: Starting at 7th level, if you spend at least 1 minute observing or interacting with another creature outside combat, you can learn certain information about its capabilities compared to your own. The DM tells you if the creature is your equal, superior, or inferior in regard to two of the following characteristics of your choice: - Strength score - Dexterity score - Constitution score - Armor Class - Current hit points - Total class levels, if any - Fighter class levels, if any Improved Combat Superiority At 10th level, your superiority dice turn into d10s. At 18th level, they turn into d12s. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:battle-master]
  - [Lvl 10] Combat Superiority (d10): When you choose this archetype at 3rd level, you learn maneuvers that are fueled by special dice called superiority dice. Maneuvers. You learn three maneuvers of your choice. Many maneuvers enhance an attack in some way. You can use only one maneuver per attack. You learn two additional maneuvers of your choice at 7th, 10th, and 15th level. Each time you learn new maneuvers, you can also replace one maneuver you know with a different one. Superiority Dice. You have four superiority dice, which are d8s. A superiority die is expended when you use it. You regain all of your expended superiority dice when you finish a short or long rest. You gain another superiority die at 7th level and one more at 15th level. Saving Throws. Some of your maneuvers require your target to make a saving throw to resist the maneuver's effects. The saving throw DC is calculated as follows: Maneuver save DC = 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Strength or Dexterity modifier (your choice) [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:battle-master]
  - [Lvl 15] Relentless: Starting at 15th level, when you roll initiative and have no superiority dice remaining, you regain 1 superiority die. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:battle-master]
  - [Lvl 18] Combat Superiority (d12): When you choose this archetype at 3rd level, you learn maneuvers that are fueled by special dice called superiority dice. Maneuvers. You learn three maneuvers of your choice. Many maneuvers enhance an attack in some way. You can use only one maneuver per attack. You learn two additional maneuvers of your choice at 7th, 10th, and 15th level. Each time you learn new maneuvers, you can also replace one maneuver you know with a different one. Superiority Dice. You have four superiority dice, which are d8s. A superiority die is expended when you use it. You regain all of your expended superiority dice when you finish a short or long rest. You gain another superiority die at 7th level and one more at 15th level. Saving Throws. Some of your maneuvers require your target to make a saving throw to resist the maneuver's effects. The saving throw DC is calculated as follows: Maneuver save DC = 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Strength or Dexterity modifier (your choice) [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:battle-master]

--- Champion (Fighter) [Player's Handbook] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Improved Critical: Beginning when you choose this archetype at 3rd level, your weapon attacks score a critical hit on a roll of 19 or 20. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:champion]
  - [Lvl 7] Remarkable Athlete: Starting at 7th level, you can add half your proficiency bonus (rounded up) to any Strength, Dexterity, or Constitution check you make that doesn't already use your proficiency bonus. In addition, when you make a running long jump, the distance you can cover increases by a number of feet equal to your Strength modifier. Additional Fighting Style At 10th level, you can choose a second option from the Fighting Style class feature. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:champion]
  - [Lvl 15] Superior Critical: Starting at 15th level, your weapon attacks score a critical hit on a roll of 18-20. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:champion]
  - [Lvl 18] Survivor: At 18th level, you attain the pinnacle of resilience in battle. At the start of each of your turns, you regain hit points equal to 5 + your Constitution modifier if you have no more than half of your hit points left. You don't gain this benefit if you have 0 hit points. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:champion]

--- Echo Knight (Fighter) [Player's Handbook] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Manifest Echo: At 3rd level, you can use a bonus action to magically manifest an echo of yourself in an unoccupied space you can see within 15 feet of you. This echo is a magical, translucent, gray image of you that lasts until it is destroyed, until you dismiss it as a bonus action, until you manifest another echo, or until you're incapacitated. Your echo has AC 14 + your proficiency bonus, 1 hit point, and immunity to all conditions. If it has to make a saving throw, it uses your saving throw bonus for the roll. It is the same size as you, and it occupies its space. On your turn, you can mentally command the echo to move up to 30 feet in any direction (no action required). If your echo is ever more than 30 feet from you at the end of your turn, it is destroyed. - As a bonus action, you can teleport, magically swapping places with your echo at a cost of 15 feet of your movement, regardless of the distance between the two of you. - When you take the Attack action on your turn, any attack you make with that action can originate from your space or the echo's space. You make this choice for each attack. - When a creature that you can see within 5 feet of your echo moves at least 5 feet away from it, you can use your reaction to make an opportunity attack against that creature as if you were in the echo's space. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:echo-knight]
  - [Lvl 3] Unleash Incarnation: At 3rd level, you can heighten your echo's fury. Whenever you take the Attack action, you can make one additional melee attack from the echo's position. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Constitution modifier (a minimum of once). You regain all expended uses when you finish a long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:echo-knight]
  - [Lvl 7] Echo Avatar: Starting at 7th level, you can temporarily transfer your consciousness to your echo. As an action, you can see through your echo's eyes and hear through its ears. During this time, you are deafened and blinded. You can sustain this effect for up to 10 minutes, and you can end it at any time (requires no action). While your echo is being used in this way, it can be up to 1,000 feet away from you without being destroyed. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:echo-knight]
  - [Lvl 10] Shadow Martyr: Starting at 10th level, you can make your echo throw itself in front of an attack directed at another creature that you can see. Before the attack roll is made, you can use your reaction to teleport the echo to an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the targeted creature. The attack roll that triggered the reaction is instead made against your echo. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a short or long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:echo-knight]
  - [Lvl 15] Reclaim Potential: By 15th level, you've learned to absorb the fleeting magic of your echo. When an echo of yours is destroyed by taking damage, you can gain a number of temporary hit points equal to 2d6 + your Constitution modifier, provided you don't already have temporary hit points. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Constitution modifier (a minimum of once). You regain all expended uses when you finish a long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:echo-knight]
  - [Lvl 18] Legion of One: At 18th level, you can use a bonus action to create two echos with your Manifest Echo feature, and these echoes can co-exist. If you try to create a third echo, the previous two echoes are destroyed. Anything you can do from one echo's position can be done from the other's instead. In addition, when you roll initiative and have no uses of your Unleash Incarnation feature left, you regain one use of that feature. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:echo-knight]

--- Eldritch Knight (Fighter) [Player's Handbook] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Weapon Bond: At 3rd level, you learn a ritual that creates a magical bond between yourself and one weapon. You perform the ritual over the course of 1 hour, which can be done during a short rest. The weapon must be within your reach throughout the ritual, at the conclusion of which you touch the weapon and forge the bond. Once you have bonded a weapon to yourself, you can't be disarmed of that weapon unless you are incapacitated. If it is on the same plane of existence, you can summon that weapon as a bonus action on your turn, causing it to teleport instantly to your hand. You can have up to two bonded weapons, but can summon only one at a time with your bonus action. If you attempt to bond with a third weapon, you must break the bond with one of the other two. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:eldritch-knight]
  - [Lvl 7] War Magic: Beginning at 7th level, when you use your action to cast a cantrip, you can make one weapon attack as a bonus action. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:eldritch-knight]
  - [Lvl 10] Eldritch Strike: At 10th level, you learn how to make your weapon strikes undercut a creature's resistance to your spells. When you hit a creature with a weapon attack, that creature has disadvantage on the next saving throw it makes against a spell you cast before the end of your next turn. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:eldritch-knight]
  - [Lvl 15] Arcane Charge: At 15th level, you gain the ability to teleport up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space you can see when you use your Action Surge. You can teleport before or after the additional action. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:eldritch-knight]
  - [Lvl 18] Improved War Magic: Starting at 18th level, when you use your action to cast a spell, you can make one weapon attack as a bonus action. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:eldritch-knight]
  - [Lvl 3] Spellcasting: Third-caster spellcasting using Intelligence; choose your spells in the Spells step.

--- Arcane Archer (Fighter) [Xanathar's Guide to Everything] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Arcane Archer Lore: At 3rd level, you learn magical theory or some of the secrets of nature – typical for practitioners of of this elven martial tradition. You choose to gain proficiency in either the Arcana or the Nature skill, and you choose to learn either the Prestidigitation or Druidcraft cantrip. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:arcane-archer]
  - [Lvl 3] Arcane Shot (2 options): You learn to use a magical effect as part of an arrow attack. You can use the effect once per turn, and the number of times you can use it grows with your levels.
  - [Lvl 7] Curving Shot: At 7th level, you learn how to direct an errant arrow toward a new target. When you make an attack roll with a magic arrow and miss, you can use a bonus action to reroll the attack roll against a different target within 60 feet of the original target. Ever-Ready Shot Starting at 15th level, your magical archery is available whenever battle starts. If you roll initiative and have no uses of Arcane Shot remaining, you regain one use of it. Arcane Shot Options The Arcane Shot feature lets you choose options for it at certain levels. The options are presented here in alphabetical order. They are all magical effects, and each one is associated with one of the schools of magic. If an option requires a saving throw, your Arcane Shot save DC equals 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Intelligence modifier. Banishing Arrow. You use abjuration magic to try to temporarily banish your target to a harmless location in the Feywild. The creature hit by the arrow must also succeed on a Charisma saving throw or be banished. While banished in this way, its speed is 0, and it is incapacitated. At the end of its next turn, the target reappears in the space it vacated or in the nearest unoccupied space if that space is occupied. After you reach 18th level in this class, a target also takes 2d6 force damage when the arrow hits it. Beguiling Arrow. Your enchantment magic causes this arrow to temporarily beguile its target. The creature hit by the arrow takes an extra 2d6 psychic damage, and choose one of your allies within 30 feet of the target. The target must succeed on a Wisdom saving throw, or it is charmed by the chosen ally until the start of your next turn. This effect ends early if the chosen ally attacks the charmed target, deals damage to it, or forces it to make a saving throw. The psychic damage increases to 4d6 when you reach 18th level in this class. Bursting Arrow. You imbue your arrow with force energy drawn from the school of evocation. The arrow detonates after your attack. Immediately after the arrow hits the creature, the target and all other creatures within 10 feet of it take 2d6 force damage each. The force damage increases to 4d6 when you reach 18th level in this class. Enfeebling Arrow. You weave necromantic magic into your arrow. The creature hit by the arrow takes an extra 2d6 necrotic damage. The target must also succeed on a Constitution saving throw, or the damage dealt by its weapon attacks is halved until the start of your next turn. The necrotic damage increases to 4d6 when you reach 18th level in this class. Grasping Arrow. When this arrow strikes its target, conjuration magic creates grasping, poisonous brambles, which wrap around the target. The creature hit by the arrow takes an extra 2d6 poison damage, its speed is reduced by 10 feet, and it takes 2d6 slashing damage the first time on each turn it moves 1 foot or more without teleporting. The target or any creature that can reach it can use its action to remove the brambles with a successful Strength (Athletics) check against your Arcane Shot save DC. Otherwise, the brambles last for 1 minute or until you use this option again. The poison damage and slashing damage both increase to 4d6 when you reach 18th level in this class. Piercing Arrow. You use transmutation magic to give your arrow an ethereal quality. When you use this option, you don’t make an attack roll for the attack. Instead, the arrow fires forward in a line, which is 1 foot wide and 30 feet long, before disappearing. The arrow passes harmlessly through objects, ignoring cover. Each creature in that line must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failed save, a creature takes damage as if it were hit by the arrow, plus an extra 1d6 piercing damage. On a successful save, a target takes half as much damage. The piercing damage increases to 2d6 when you reach 18th level in this class. Seeking Arrow. Using divination magic, you grant your arrow the ability to seek out your target, allowing the arrow to curve and twist its path in search of its prey. When you use this option, you don’t make an attack roll for the attack. Instead, choose one creature you have seen in the past minute. The arrow flies toward that creature, moving around corners if necessary and ignoring three-quarters cover and half cover. If the target is within the weapon’s range and there is a path large enough for the arrow to travel to the target, the target must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failed save, it takes damage as if it were hit by the arrow, plus an extra 1d6 force damage, and you learn the target’s current location. On a successful save, the target takes half as much damage, and you don’t learn its location. The force damage increases to 2d6 when you reach 18th level in this class. Shadow Arrow. You weave illusion magic into your arrow, causing it to occlude your foe’s vision with shadows. The creature hit by the arrow takes an extra 2d6 psychic damage, and it must succeed on a Wisdom saving throw or be unable to see anything farther than 5 feet away until the start of your next turn. The psychic damage increases to 4d6 when you reach 18th level in this class. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:arcane-archer]
  - [Lvl 7] Magic Arrow: At 7th level, you gain the ability to infuse arrows with magic. Whenever you fire a nonmagical arrow from a shortbow or longbow, you can make it magical for the purpose of overcoming resistance and immunity to nonmagical attacks and damage. The magic fades from the arrow immediately after it hits or misses its target. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:arcane-archer]
  - [Lvl 7] Arcane Shot (3 options): At 3rd level, you learn to unleash special magical effects with some of your shots. When you gain this feature, you learn two Arcane Shot options of your choice (see "Arcane Shot Options" below). Once per turn when you fire an arrow from a shortbow or longbow as part of the Attack action, you can apply one of your Arcane Shot options to that arrow. You decide to use the option when the arrow hits, unless the option doesn’t involve an attack roll. You have two uses of this ability, and you regain all expended uses of it when you finish a short or long rest. You gain an additional Arcane Shot option of your choice when you reach certain levels in this class: 7th, 10th, 15th, and 18th level. Each option also improves when you become an 18th-level fighter. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:arcane-archer]
  - [Lvl 10] Arcane Shot (4 options): At 3rd level, you learn to unleash special magical effects with some of your shots. When you gain this feature, you learn two Arcane Shot options of your choice (see "Arcane Shot Options" below). Once per turn when you fire an arrow from a shortbow or longbow as part of the Attack action, you can apply one of your Arcane Shot options to that arrow. You decide to use the option when the arrow hits, unless the option doesn’t involve an attack roll. You have two uses of this ability, and you regain all expended uses of it when you finish a short or long rest. You gain an additional Arcane Shot option of your choice when you reach certain levels in this class: 7th, 10th, 15th, and 18th level. Each option also improves when you become an 18th-level fighter. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:arcane-archer]
  - [Lvl 15] Ready Shot: (no description on site)
  - [Lvl 15] Arcane Shot (5 options): At 3rd level, you learn to unleash special magical effects with some of your shots. When you gain this feature, you learn two Arcane Shot options of your choice (see "Arcane Shot Options" below). Once per turn when you fire an arrow from a shortbow or longbow as part of the Attack action, you can apply one of your Arcane Shot options to that arrow. You decide to use the option when the arrow hits, unless the option doesn’t involve an attack roll. You have two uses of this ability, and you regain all expended uses of it when you finish a short or long rest. You gain an additional Arcane Shot option of your choice when you reach certain levels in this class: 7th, 10th, 15th, and 18th level. Each option also improves when you become an 18th-level fighter. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:arcane-archer]
  - [Lvl 18] Arcane Shot (6 options: At 3rd level, you learn to unleash special magical effects with some of your shots. When you gain this feature, you learn two Arcane Shot options of your choice (see "Arcane Shot Options" below). Once per turn when you fire an arrow from a shortbow or longbow as part of the Attack action, you can apply one of your Arcane Shot options to that arrow. You decide to use the option when the arrow hits, unless the option doesn’t involve an attack roll. You have two uses of this ability, and you regain all expended uses of it when you finish a short or long rest. You gain an additional Arcane Shot option of your choice when you reach certain levels in this class: 7th, 10th, 15th, and 18th level. Each option also improves when you become an 18th-level fighter. --- Cavalier (Fighter) [Xanathar's Guide to Everything] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Bonus Proficiency: When you gain this feature, choose a skill or a language of your choice.
  - [Lvl 3] Born to the Saddle: Starting at 3rd level, your mastery as a rider becomes apparent. You have advantage on saving throws made to avoid falling off your mount. If you fall off your mount and descend no more than 10 feet, you can land on your feet if you’re not incapacitated. Finally, mounting or dismounting a creature costs you only 5 feet of movement, rather than half your speed. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:cavalier]
  - [Lvl 3] Unwavering Mark: Starting at 3rd level, you can menace your foes, foiling their attacks and punishing them for harming others. When you hit a creature with a melee weapon attack, you can mark the creature until the end of your next turn. This effect ends early if you are incapacitated or you die, or if someone else marks the creature. While it is within 5 feet of you, a creature marked by you has disadvantage on any attack roll that doesn't target you. In addition, if a creature marked by you deals damage to anyone other than you, you can make a special melee weapon attack against the marked creature as a bonus action on your next turn. You have advantage on the attack roll, and if it hits, the attack's weapon deals extra damage to the target equal to half your fighter level. Regardless of the number of creatures you mark, you can make this special attack a number of times equal to your Strength modifier (a minimum of once), and you regain all expended uses of it when you finish a long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:cavalier]
  - [Lvl 7] Warding Maneuver: At 7th level, you learn to fend off strikes directed at you, your mount, or other creatures nearby. If you or a creature you can see within 5 feet of you is hit by an attack, you can roll 1d8 as a reaction if you're wielding a melee weapon or a shield. Roll the die, and add the number rolled to the target's AC against that attack. If the attack still hits, the target has resistance against the attack's damage. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Constitution modifier (a minimum of once), and you regain all expended uses of it when you finish a long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:cavalier]
  - [Lvl 10] Hold the Line: At 10th level, you become a master of locking down your enemies. Creatures provoke an opportunity attack from you when they move 5 feet or more while within your reach, and if you hit a creature with an opportunity attack, the target's speed is reduced to 0 until the end of the current turn. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:cavalier]
  - [Lvl 15] Ferocious Charger: Starting at 15th level, you can run down your foes, whether you're mounted or not. If you move at least 10 feet in a straight line right before attacking a creature and you hit it with the attack, that target must succeed on a Strength saving throw (DC 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Strength modifier) or be knocked prone. You can use this feature only once on each of your turns. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:cavalier]
  - [Lvl 18] Vigilant Defender: Starting at 18th level, you respond to danger with extraordinary vigilance. In combat, you get a special reaction that you can take once on every creature's turn, except your turn. You can use this special reaction only to make an opportunity attack, and you can't use it on the same turn that you take your normal reaction. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:cavalier]
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
  (none)
Tracked resources (resourceGrants):
  (none)
Choices (choiceGroups):
  - Skill or language (choose 1, lvl 3)
Catalog link: subclass:cavalier

--- Samurai (Fighter) [Xanathar's Guide to Everything] ---
Features / traits (featureGrants):
  - [Lvl 3] Bonus Proficiency: When you gain this feature, choose a skill or a language of your choice.
  - [Lvl 3] Fighting Spirit: Starting at 3rd level, your intensity in battle can shield you and help you strike true. As a bonus action on your turn, you can give yourself advantage on all weapon attack rolls until the end of the current turn. When you do so, you also gain 5 temporary hit points. The number of hit points increases when you reach certain levels in this class, increasing to 10 at 10th level and 15 at 15th level. You can use this feature three times. You regain all expended uses of it when you finish a long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:samurai]
  - [Lvl 7] Elegant Courtier: Starting at 7th level, your discipline and attention to detail allow you to excel in social situations. Whenever you make a Charisma (Persuasion) check, you gain a bonus to the check equal to your Wisdom modifier. Your self-control also causes you to gain proficiency in Wisdom saving throws. If you already have this proficiency, you instead gain proficiency in Intelligence or Charisma saving throws (your choice). [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:samurai]
  - [Lvl 10] Tireless Spirit: Starting at 10th level, when you roll initiative and have no uses of Fighting Spirit remaining, you regain one use. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:samurai]
  - [Lvl 15] Rapid Strike: Starting at 15th level, you learn to trade accuracy for swift strikes. If you take the Attack action on your turn and have advantage on an attack roll against one of the targets, you can forgo the advantage for that roll to make an additional weapon attack against that target, as part of the same action. You can do so no more than once per turn. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:samurai]
  - [Lvl 18] Strength Before Death: Starting at 18th level, your fighting spirit can delay the grasp of death. If you take damage that reduces you to 0 hit points, you can use your reaction to delay falling unconscious, and you can immediately take an extra turn. While you have 0 hit points during that extra turn, taking damage causes death saving throw failures as normal, and three death saving throw failures can still kill you. When the extra turn ends, you fall unconscious if you still have 0 hit points. Once you use this feature, you can’t use it again until you finish a long rest. [Source: https://dnd5e.wikidot.com/fighter:samurai]
Ability / proficiency grants (statModifiers):
